### [57] [Fixed-Time Convergence of Time-Varying Neurodynamic Systems for Mixed Variational Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00002)
*Vajahat Karim Khan,Vijendra Kumar Varshney,Md. Kalimuddin Ahmad*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对混合变分不等式问题（MVIs）提出了一类新型固定时间收敛神经动力学方法，包括时间变分近端神经动力学模型（TVPNMs），在强伪单调性和Lipschitz连续性假设下，利用Lyapunov稳定性理论严格证明了任意初始条件下的固定时间收敛性，并通过时变系数设计显著加速收敛速度，同时分析了方法在有界噪声干扰下的鲁棒性，并展示了在复合优化和极小化极大优化问题中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经动力学方法在求解混合变分不等式问题时收敛速度有限，难以保证固定时间内的收敛性，且缺乏对时变系数的系统设计来加速收敛，同时也需要进一步研究在噪声干扰下的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出了一类一阶近端神经动力学模型（PNMs），包括时间变分近端神经动力学模型（TVPNMs），通过设计时变系数实现固定时间收敛；利用Lyapunov稳定性理论在强伪单调性和Lipschitz连续性假设下进行严格的收敛性和稳定性分析；推导了时变神经动力学模型驻留时间的显式上界。

Result: 所提方法在任意初始条件下均能实现固定时间收敛，时变系数的策略设计显著提升了收敛速度；给出了驻留时间的显式上界；方法在存在有界噪声干扰时仍保持鲁棒性；通过复合优化和极小化极大优化问题验证了方法的适用性；数值实验证实了方法的有效性和收敛性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的固定时间收敛神经动力学方法为求解混合变分不等式问题提供了高效且鲁棒的解决方案，时变系数的设计为加速收敛提供了新思路，且方法具有良好的泛化应用前景。

Abstract: This paper proposes novel fixed-time (FXT) convergent neurodynamic approaches for solving mixed variational inequality problems (MVIs). A class of first-order proximal neurodynamic models (PNMs), including time-varying proximal neurodynamic models (TVPNMs), is developed to guarantee FXT convergence to the solution of MVIs from arbitrary initial conditions. Rigorous convergence and stability analyses are established under the assumptions of strong pseudomonotonicity and Lipschitz continuity, using Lyapunov stability theory. The proposed methods exhibit FXT convergence from any initial point, with convergence speed significantly enhanced through the strategic design of time-varying coefficients. Explicit upper bounds on the settling time are derived for the time-varying neurodynamic models. In addition, the robustness of the proposed approaches against bounded noise disturbances is analyzed. The applicability of the proposed framework is further demonstrated for composite optimization problems and minimax optimization problems. Also, numerical examples are presented to demonstrate the effectiveness and convergence behavior of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [58] [A Homotopy Framework for Constrained Multiobjective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00003)
*Olaoluwa Ogunleye,Guangming Yao,Jianhua Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于同伦的框架，用于计算多目标优化问题的KKT点。同伦映射将易解系统连续变形为KKT条件，生成确定性且保结构的延续路径。在温和正则性假设下，建立了从可行域内部任意初始点出发的同伦轨迹全局收敛到Pareto驻点解的性质。数值实验表明该方法即使从不可行点初始化也能稳健收敛。采用高效的预测-校正延续策略追踪同伦路径。与传统标量化和NSGA-II的对比实验展示了其竞争性的计算效率和一致解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标优化问题中，传统标量化和进化算法存在权重敏感性、解分布不均或计算成本高等问题，缺乏兼具全局收敛保证和计算效率的确定性方法。现有同伦方法多针对单目标优化，缺乏面向多目标KKT系统的系统性框架。

Method: 构造一个参数化的同伦映射，将初始易解系统（如可行域内点的最优性条件）连续变形为原多目标问题的KKT条件。利用预测-校正延续算法（如Euler预测+Newton校正）追踪同伦路径，在路径上逐步获得Pareto驻点。理论分析基于正则性假设和隐函数定理证明路径存在性和全局收敛性。

Result: 理论层面：在温和正则性假设下证明了对任意可行域内初始点，同伦轨迹全局收敛到Pareto驻点。数值层面：在基准问题上与标量化方法（加权和法、ε-约束法）和NSGA-II对比，展现了竞争的计算效率和一致解质量；即使从不可行点初始化也能稳健收敛，超越理论保证范围。

Conclusion: 同伦框架为约束多目标优化提供了一种兼具全局收敛理论保证和实际计算鲁棒性的确定性方法，在效率和解质量上与主流方法相当甚至更优。未来工作可扩展到更一般的问题设置（如非光滑、大尺度）并开发自适应参数策略。

Abstract: We develop a homotopy-based framework for computing Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) points of multiobjective optimization problems. The proposed homotopy map continuously deforms an easily solvable system into the KKT conditions associated with the multiobjective problem, yielding a deterministic and structure-preserving continuation path. Under mild regularity assumptions, we establish global convergence of the homotopy trajectory to a Pareto-stationary solution for any initial point chosen in the interior of the feasible region. In numerical experiments, the method exhibits robust convergence even when initialized from nonfeasible points, indicating stability beyond the theoretical guarantees. Efficient predictor-corrector continuation strategies are employed to trace the homotopy path. Numerical results on benchmark problems compare the proposed approach with classical scalarization methods and the evolutionary algorithm NSGA-II, demonstrating competitive computational efficiency and consistent solution quality. These results highlight the effectiveness of the homotopy framework for constrained multiobjective optimization and motivate extensions to more general problem settings and adaptive parameter strategies.

</details>


### [59] [Optimization-based One-side Boundary Control of LWR Traffic Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00004)
*Eryn Vaid,Maria Teresa Chiri,Roberto Guglielmi,Gennaro Notomista*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类基于优化的宏观交通流模型边界控制方法，通过单个边界控制同时实现稳定性和不变性，利用Lyapunov泛函和边界控制障碍泛函定义控制器集合，并通过凸优化选择控制输入，给出了确保最优控制问题存在性的充分条件，并通过仿真验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的宏观交通流边界控制通常分别处理稳定性或不变性问题，缺乏能够同时保证系统稳定到期望状态且维持状态在安全子集内的统一框架。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索通过单一边界控制同时实现这两类目标的可行性。

Method: 1) 利用Lyapunov泛函定义能够将系统稳定到期望状态的控制器集合；2) 利用边界控制障碍泛函定义确保状态前向不变性的控制器集合；3) 通过凸优化问题的求解，从两集合的交集中选取控制输入；4) 推导了一类通用交通流通量函数下最优边界控制问题存在性的充分条件。

Result: 推导出了确保同时实现稳定性和不变性的最优边界控制问题存在性的充分条件，并通过多个交通流通量函数的仿真展示了所提出优化控制器的行为。

Conclusion: 基于优化的边界控制方法能够有效结合Lyapunov泛函和边界控制障碍泛函，在一维宏观交通流模型中通过单一边界控制同时实现系统稳定性和状态不变性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the feasibility of a class of optimization-based boundary control of one-dimensional macroscopic traffic flow models, where stability and invariance are achieved by a single boundary control. We define the sets of controllers to stabilize the system to a desired state via Lyapunov functionals, and to ensure forward invariance of a desired subset via boundary control barrier functionals. The control input is then selected from the intersection of those sets via a convex optimization problem. We determine sufficient conditions to ensure the existence of an optimal boundary control problem achieving both stability and invariance for a generic traffic flux function. Simulation results showcase the behavior of the proposed optimization-based controller applied to conservation laws with several traffic flow functions.

</details>

*Mrinal Kanti Roychowdhury*

TL;DR: 本文对球面几何上的最优离散量化进行了解析研究，针对单位球面S²上三种高度对称的均匀配置模型，推导出了最优量化器和均方误差的闭式表达式，并提出了块中点原理。

(III) For a single small circle at latitude $φ_0$, analogous formulas are proved and curvature is shown to reduce distortion by a factor $\cos^2φ_0$ while preserving the $n^{-2}$ decay rate. Abstract: Independent sample generation is the prevailing paradigm in modern diffusion-based generative models of AI. We ask a different question: can samples \emph{coordinate} through shared population statistics to transport probability mass more efficiently? The coupling converts distribution matching into a McKean--Vlasov extension of the stochastic optimal transport problem, unifying generative modeling and multi-agent control under the same Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman/Kolmogorov--Fokker--Planck duality. We identify two analytically tractable regimes: a Linear--Quadratic--Gaussian (LQG) benchmark in which the infinite-dimensional mean-field system reduces to a finite set of Riccati and linear ODEs, and a Gaussian-mixture regime governed by a piecewise-constant protocol that preserves closed-form solvability. Due to the computational complexity of the problem, we develop an Adaptive Variable Neighborhood Search (AVNS) that combines established routing operators with novel mechanisms for covering decisions. Furthermore, we apply our AVNS to a real-world case study based on the 2024 flash floods in Afghanistan. Abstract: Decentralized stochastic control problems are difficult to study due to information structure dependent subtleties, which prevent many classical methods in stochastic control from being applicable. In this paper we consider such problems with general standard Borel spaces under two related information structures. (a) the one-step delayed information sharing pattern (OSDISP) where agents share their information with one-step delay, and (b) the $K$-step periodic information sharing pattern (KSPISP), where information is shared periodically. It is known that OSDISP and KSPISP problems admit a centralized reduction where the agents view the problem from the perspective of a centralized controller that uses the common information to prescribe function valued actions (local policies) which map each agent's private information to an optimal action in the original problem. We provide rigorous approximation results and performance bounds for the KSPISP and OSDISP problems, which results from replacing the full common information by a finite sliding window of information and we establish near optimality of such policies. The latter depends on a predictor stability condition in expected total variation. As a further contribution, we show that under the information structures provided, corresponding Q-learning algorithms (in quantized or finite memory forms) converge asymptotically to near optimal solutions. While restrictive and hypothetical conditions have been presented in the literature, our contributions are thus to provide, to our knowledge, the first explicit conditions and rigorous approximation and learning results for such decentralized problems with general spaces.

</details>

Abstract: We develop a structure-preserving solution framework for the optimal control of the time-dependent Maxwell's equations. Building on a well-posedness theory for a weak form of the forward problem, we first analyze a forward solver that couples Nédélec and Raviart--Thomas finite elements with Crank--Nicolson time stepping. The solver preserves the de~Rham structure, enforces a discrete Gauss law, exactly satisfies a per-time-step energy balance, and converges to the weak solution under low regularity assumptions on the problem data, which are dictated by the optimal control setting. To control the Maxwell system, we add the curl of a space-time current density as a source to Ampére's law. The curl form yields charge conservation without auxiliary constraints. We prove the well-posedness and continuity of the control-to-state map, derive the adjoint system and a gradient representation for a tracking-type objective functional, and formulate a discrete optimization scheme that inherits structure preservation from the forward solver. Our discrete stationarity conditions are consistent with their continuous counterparts, and the discrete optimal controls converge, with mesh and time refinements, to the continuous optima. We demonstrate the merits of our optimal control formulation and the theoretical developments by numerically solving a series of source-cloaking model problems.

Abstract: This paper presents a unified derivation of transversality conditions in optimal control problems using exact penalty functions. The key regularity condition is that the origin is uniformly separated from the subdifferential of the penalty function in a neighborhood of the admissible set. This condition, hereafter referred to as the Unified Separation Condition (USC), generalizes the classical Mangasarian-Fromovitz condition for inequalities and linear independence of gradients for equalities; in the smooth case, these classical conditions are equivalent to USC, as shown via Gordan's theorem. The USC remains applicable even when constraint functions are nondifferentiable, where classical constraint qualifications are not defined. Assuming exactness, we derive transversality conditions for all major cases: fixed and free terminal time, equality and inequality constraints, moving manifolds, and free left endpoint. Remarkably, this approach yields these classical results in a concise and transparent manner, avoiding the need for constructing cones of endpoint variations or applying separation theorems. The theoretical results are complemented by a numerical implementation applied to the time-optimal control of a harmonic oscillator. The numerical implementation converges to the exact solution obtained via Pontryagin's maximum principle combined with transversality conditions, confirming the consistency and practical applicability of the proposed methodology.

Abstract: We present a focused introduction to exact penalty methods for nonlinear programs and mathematical programs with equilibrium constraints (MPECs), emphasizing their connection to modern error bound theory. The goal is twofold. First, we explain how classical optimality conditions can be interpreted through exact penalization, and why such results typically rely on constraint regularity conditions that can be understood as error bounds on perturbations of feasible sets. We then highlight how recent developments based on subanalytic geometry and Lojasiewicz-type inequalities extend this framework beyond classical regularity assumptions, enabling exact penalization under broader analytic conditions.

Particular attention is given to fractional-order penalties arising from Lojasiewicz error bounds, as well as to improved formulations for special problem classes where sharper exponents can be obtained. These developments provide both theoretical insight and practical guidance for analyzing and solving challenging constrained optimization problems.

The goal is twofold. First, we explain why a direct application of standard optimality conditions -- based on reformulating MPECs via KKT systems or differentiable exact penalty functions -- is often inadequate, as such approaches typically require strong and restrictive assumptions, including nondegeneracy and smoothness conditions.
  Second, we develop a first-principles framework for analyzing MPECs by focusing on the geometric structure of the feasible region. In particular, we study stationarity concepts and provide a detailed characterization of the tangent cone at feasible points, which leads to appropriate constraint qualifications tailored to MPECs. These results form the foundation for rigorous first-order analysis and clarify the relationship between the original MPEC formulation and its KKT-based representation, offering practical guidance for handling these inherently challenging optimization problems.

</details>


### [69] [Controlling the Swarm: Sparse Actuation and Collision Avoidance under Stochastic Delay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00395)
*Jiguang Yu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对延迟随机领导者-跟随者粒子系统，提出了一个统一的有限N框架。通过引入增广Lyapunov泛函解决了数学适定性问题，并构建了机会约束最优控制问题，揭示了在延迟随机群体中，增加直接驱动并非严格最优的资源分配悖论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界多智能体部署受限于执行器可用性有限、异构通信延迟和环境噪声等严重约束，而经典群体模型未能同时处理这些机制的交织作用。本文旨在解决这些约束共存下的数学适定性及最优控制问题。

Method: 采用延迟随机领导者-跟随者粒子系统，结合拓扑通信、奇异排斥和有界稀疏领导者驱动；通过增广Lyapunov泛函同时强制执行严格碰撞屏障并闭合均匀Gronwall估计；在此基础上构建了自由终端时间、机会约束的最优控制问题。

Result: 时间稀疏的bang-off-bang领导者驱动相比连续基线显著降低了控制代价；揭示了领导者密度非单调敏感性；发现了延迟随机群体中增加直接驱动并非严格最优的资源分配悖论。

Conclusion: 在延迟随机群体中，增加更多直接驱动并非严格最优，这突显了协同控制中一个高度非平凡的资源分配悖论。

Abstract: Classical flocking models demonstrate how local interactions generate emergent order, but real-world multi-agent deployments are bound by severe constraints: limited actuator availability, heterogeneous communication latencies, and environmental noise. In this talk, we present a unified finite-N framework that tackles the interplay of these exact mechanisms. We study a delayed stochastic leader-follower particle system featuring topological communication, singular repulsion, and bounded sparse leader actuation.
  A central challenge in such systems is mathematical well-posedness, as discontinuous communication laws and singular repulsions clash with standard strong Ito frameworks. We resolve this by introducing an augmented Lyapunov functional that simultaneously enforces a strict collision barrier and closes a uniform Gronwall estimate. Building on this rigorous foundation, we formulate a free-terminal-time, chance-constrained optimal control problem. We show that temporally sparse, bang-off-bang leader actuation not only drastically reduces control effort compared to continuous baselines, but also reveals non-monotone sensitivities to leader density. Ultimately, we demonstrate that in delayed stochastic swarms, adding more direct actuation is not strictly optimal -- highlighting a highly non-trivial resource allocation paradox in cooperative control.

</details>


### [70] [Riemannian Optimization over Symmetric Positive Definite Matrices with the Alpha-Procrustes Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00396)
*Derun Zhou,Keisuke Yano,Mahito Sugiyama*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对SPD流形上黎曼优化中因条件数恶化导致的优化困难问题，提出了Alpha-Procrustes (AP)几何框架。当α=1时，黎曼度量算子的特征值均匀有界，不受底层SPD矩阵条件数影响，从而为病态SPD矩阵的黎曼优化提供了鲁棒的几何框架。数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在SPD流形上，常用的AI度量和BW度量在SPD矩阵病态时会导致黎曼Hessian的条件数恶化，即使欧几里得Hessian保持良好条件数，优化仍可能变得困难。本文旨在解决这一问题。

Method: 提出Alpha-Procrustes (AP)几何框架，该框架推广了Log-Euclidean度量（α=0）和BW度量（α=1/2）。重点分析α=1的情形，证明此时黎曼度量算子的所有特征值均匀有界，与底层SPD矩阵无关。

Result: 当α=1时，AP几何的黎曼度量算子特征值均匀有界；在欧几里得Hessian满足均匀谱界假设下，相应的黎曼Hessian的所有特征值也均匀有界，与底层SPD矩阵无关。

Conclusion: α=1的AP几何为涉及病态SPD矩阵的黎曼优化问题提供了一个鲁棒的几何框架，有效缓解了因SPD矩阵条件数恶化导致的优化困难。

Abstract: In Riemannian optimization, it is well known that the condition number of the Riemannian Hessian at an optimum strongly influences the asymptotic convergence behavior of optimization algorithms. On the manifold of symmetric positive definite (SPD) matrices, several commonly used metrics for optimization, such as the Affine-Invariant (AI) and Bures--Wasserstein (BW) metrics, tend to become ill-conditioned as the underlying SPD matrix becomes ill-conditioned. As a result, even when the Euclidean Hessian remains uniformly well-conditioned on the SPD manifold, optimization may still become difficult near an optimum associated with an ill-conditioned SPD matrix. In this paper, we address this issue through the Alpha-Procrustes (AP) geometry on the SPD manifold. This geometry generalizes several well-known metrics, including the Log-Euclidean (LE) metric for \(α=0\) and the BW metric for \(α=1/2\). We first show that, when \(α=1\), all eigenvalues of the Riemannian metric operator induced by the AP geometry are uniformly bounded independently of the underlying SPD matrix. Therefore, under the assumption that the Euclidean Hessian satisfies the uniform spectral bounds, all the eigenvalues of the corresponding Riemannian Hessian are uniformly bounded independently of the underlying SPD matrix. Consequently, the case \(α=1\) provides a robust geometric framework for several Riemannian optimization problems involving ill-conditioned SPD matrices. Finally, we validate our theoretical findings through extensive numerical experiments across a range of applications.

</details>


### [71] [On the Distribution of Unweighted Minimum Knapsack Instances with Large SOS Rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00594)
*Adam Kurpisz,Lucas Slot,Mikhail Zaytsev*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了无权重最小背包问题（MK）的平方和（SOS）层级秩，给出了参数q在全范围内的上下界。主要结果表明：当n-q ≤ O(1)时SOS秩为常数；当q ≤ O(1)且q指数接近整数时需要线性秩；但线性秩极为罕见——对q施加高斯扰动后，期望SOS秩仅为O(√n log(n/σ))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解平方和（SOS）层级在求解无权重最小背包问题（MK）时的局限性，探究SOS秩在参数q的整个取值范围内的典型行为，回答“困难实例是常态还是特例”这一核心问题。

Method: 理论分析：推导无权重MK问题在参数q全范围内的SOS秩上下界；对q ≤ O(1)情形采用平滑分析（smoothed analysis），对q施加高斯扰动后分析期望SOS秩。

Result: (1) 当n-q ≤ O(1)时，SOS秩为常数；(2) 当q ≤ O(1)且q指数接近整数时需要线性秩；(3) 平滑分析下，对q施加均值为0、方差为σ²的高斯扰动后，期望SOS秩为O(√n log(n/σ))，表明线性秩极为罕见。

Conclusion: 无权重MK问题的SOS秩在大多数参数设定下为次线性，线性秩仅在q指数接近整数时出现，属于罕见情况。这为理解SOS层级在布尔优化中的实际能力提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We analyze the sum-of-squares rank of unweighted instances of the Minimum Knapsack (MK) problem, i.e., minimization of $\sum_{i=1}^n x_i$ for 0/1 variables under the constraint $\sum_{i=1}^n x_i \geq q$, with $q \in \mathbb{R}$. Such instances have long served as a testbed for understanding the limitations of lift-and-project methods in Boolean optimization. For example, both the Lovász-Schrijver and Sherali-Adams hierarchies require (maximal) rank $n$ to solve them, already when $q=1/2$ is constant. The SOS hierarchy requires only \emph{sublinear} rank $O(\sqrt{n})$ to solve unweighted MK when $q=1/2$. On the other hand, when $q$ is allowed to vary with~$n$, the SOS rank of the problem may become linear. Interestingly, this is known to happen both when $q$ is large, and when $q$ is very small ($0<q \leq 2^{-n}$). This raises the question of whether we should think of hard instances of unweighted MK as being typical for the SOS hierarchy, or as a consequence of very specific choices of the threshold parameter $q$.
  In this paper, we address this question by showing new upper and lower bounds on the SOS rank of unweighted MK in the whole regime of the parameter $q$. For $n-q \leq O(1)$, we show that the SOS rank is constant. In contrast, when $q \leq O(1)$, a linear rank is needed if $q$ is exponentially close to an integer. As our main positive result, we show that linear rank is very rare for $q \leq O(1)$. This can be expressed in the language of smoothed analysis: after perturbing $q$ by a Gaussian with mean $0$ and variance $σ^2$, the expected SOS rank of MK is $O(\sqrt{n} \log (n/σ))$.

</details>


### [72] [Optimal Merton's Problem under Multivariate Affine Volterra Models with Jumps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00688)
*Sigui Brice Dro,Emmanuel Gnabeyeu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究多资产跳跃扩散金融市场中指数、幂和对数效用投资者的Merton投资组合选择问题，在多元Volterra-Heston跳跃模型框架下，通过构造Riccati倒向随机微分方程（Riccati BSDEJ）的解族，推导出半闭形式的最优策略和最优值函数，并通过二维粗糙Heston模型的数值实验展示了路径粗糙度和跳跃成分的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Merton投资组合问题在非马尔可夫、非半鞅的Volterra随机环境下难以直接应用传统随机控制方法，亟需发展新的分析框架来处理带跳跃的多资产市场中的最优投资策略问题。

Method: 采用鞅最优性原理，构造一族由带跳跃的Riccati倒向随机微分方程（Riccati BSDEJ）刻画的超鞅过程，将问题转化为求解依赖于时间的多元Riccati-Volterra方程和Riccati BSDEJ。

Result: 获得了Merton问题在指数、幂和对数效用下的半闭形式最优策略，该策略依赖于时间依赖的多元Riccati-Volterra方程的解；最优值函数通过Riccati BSDEJ的解表达。二维粗糙Heston模型的数值实验揭示了路径粗糙度和跳跃成分对值函数和最优策略的影响。

Conclusion: 所提出的Riccati BSDEJ方法有效解决了非马尔可夫、非半鞅框架下的多资产跳跃市场Merton投资组合问题，为Volterra随机环境下带跳的最优投资策略提供了可行的分析工具。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with portfolio selection for an investor with exponential, power, and logarithmic utility in multi-asset financial markets allowing jumps. We investigate the classical Merton's portfolio optimization problem in a Volterra stochastic environment described by a multivariate Volterra--Heston model with jumps driven by an independent Poisson random measure. Owing to the non-Markovian and non-semimartingale nature of the model, classical stochastic control techniques are not directly applicable. Instead, the problem is tackled using the martingale optimality principle by constructing a family of supermartingale processes characterized via solutions to an original Riccati backward stochastic differential equation with jumps (Riccati BSDEJ).The resulting optimal strategies for Merton's problems are derived in semi-closed form depending on the solutions to time-dependent multivariate Riccati-Volterra equations, while the optimal value is expressed using the solution to this original Riccati BSDEJ. Numerical experiments on a two-dimensional rough Heston model illustrate the impact of both path roughness and jumps components on the value function and optimal strategies in the Merton problem.

</details>


### [73] [A Line-search-free Method for Adaptive Decentralized Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00711)
*Xiaokai Chen,Ilya Kuruzov,Gesualdo Scutari*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需全局调参和线搜索的完全分布式自适应步长算法，每个agent仅利用本地过去迭代点和梯度来更新步长，实现了对凸和强凸目标函数的次线性/线性收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式优化方法要么需要全局问题参数来调步长（不实用），要么需要昂贵的每轮线搜索（需访问局部函数值），缺乏一种完全分布式的免调参自适应步长方法。

Method: 设计了一种新的Lyapunov函数，从中自然推导出自适应步长规则：每个agent基于连续梯度计算局部曲率估计，选择保证下降的最大步长，无需额外函数评估和全局调参。

Result: 凸目标下达到次线性收敛率，强凸目标下达到线性收敛率；标准基准实验上持续优于现有自适应和非自适应方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的免线搜索、完全分布式的自适应步长算法在理论和实践上均优于现有方法，为分布式优化提供了一种实用高效的解决方案。

Abstract: We study decentralized optimization over networks where agents cooperatively minimize a smooth (strongly) convex sum of local losses while communicating only with immediate neighbors. Prevailing decentralized methods require either centralized knowledge of global problem and network parameters for stepsize tuning--hence impractical, or costly per-iteration line-searches that demand access to local function values. We propose line-search-free, fully decentralized algorithms in which each agent adapts its stepsize using only past local iterates and gradients--with no extra function evaluations and no global tuning. The key technical ingredient is a new Lyapunov function, from which a natural adaptive stepsize rule emerges: at each iteration, each agent selects the largest stepsize that guarantees descent, based solely on a local curvature estimate built from successive gradients. The proposed algorithms enjoy strong theoretical guarantees: sublinear convergence rates for merely convex objectives and linear rates under strong convexity. Numerical experiments on standard benchmarks show consistent improvements over the state of the art, both adaptive and non-adaptive.

</details>


### [74] [Randomized Subspace Nesterov Accelerated Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00740)
*Gaku Omiya,Pierre-Louis Poirion,Akiko Takeda*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对随机子空间方法缺乏加速优化理论的问题，提出了使用矩阵光滑性和草图矩假设的随机子空间Nesterov加速梯度方法，建立了加速的预言机复杂度保证，并明确了矩阵光滑性和草图分布对复杂度的影响，为比较不同草图族和识别何时加速优于全维Nesterov方法提供了统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nesterov加速在全梯度和坐标方法中已充分研究，但对仅使用投影梯度信息的通用子空间草图方法，如何获得加速效果且在预言机复杂度上超越全维Nesterov加速方法，技术上存在显著挑战。

Method: 提出三序列公式适应矩阵光滑性，在矩阵光滑性和通用草图矩假设下开发随机子空间Nesterov加速梯度方法，涵盖光滑凸和光滑强凸优化场景。

Result: 建立了加速的预言机复杂度保证，明确了矩阵光滑性和草图分布如何影响复杂度，为比较草图族和判断何时加速优于全维方法提供了统一理论基础。

Conclusion: 所提出的随机子空间Nesterov加速方法在预言机复杂度上可超越全维Nesterov方法，且理论框架统一了多种草图族的比较分析。

Abstract: Randomized-subspace methods reduce the cost of first-order optimization by using only low-dimensional projected-gradient information, a feature that is attractive in forward-mode automatic differentiation and communication-limited settings. While Nesterov acceleration is well understood for full-gradient and coordinate-based methods, obtaining accelerated methods for general subspace sketches that use only projected-gradient information and can improve over full-dimensional Nesterov acceleration in oracle complexity is technically nontrivial.
  We develop randomized-subspace Nesterov accelerated gradient methods for smooth convex and smooth strongly convex optimization under matrix smoothness and generic sketch moment assumptions. The key technical ingredient is a three-sequence formulation tailored to matrix smoothness, which recovers the corresponding classical Nesterov methods in the full-dimensional case. The resulting theory establishes accelerated oracle-complexity guarantees and makes explicit how matrix smoothness and the sketch distribution enter the complexity. It also provides a unified basis for comparing sketch families and identifying when randomized-subspace acceleration improves over full-dimensional Nesterov acceleration in oracle complexity.

</details>

### [75] [FedACT: Concurrent Federated Intelligence across Heterogeneous Data Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00011)
*Md Sirajul Islam,Isabelle G Chapman,N I Md Ashafuddula,Xu Yuan,Li Chen,Nian-Feng Tzeng,Klara Nahrstedt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedACT是一种面向多联邦学习任务场景的异构资源感知设备调度方法，通过对齐评分机制和参与公平性策略，动态分配异构设备以最小化平均任务完成时间，实验表明其相比现有方法可将平均JCT降低最多8.3倍，模型准确率提升最多44.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中多个机器学习任务需同时在共享设备池中训练，而单一FL优化技术直接应用于多FL系统会因设备异构性和资源低效导致次优性能。为解决这一关键开放挑战，需要设计高效的异构设备调度方法。

Method: 提出FedACT方法，包含三个核心组件：(1) 对齐评分机制，评估设备可用资源与任务资源需求的兼容性；(2) 参与公平性策略，确保设备在各任务间的均衡贡献；(3) 最优调度计划，优先分配高对齐得分的设备同时保障任务间公平参与。

Result: 在多种FL任务和基准数据集上的实验表明，与最先进基线方法相比，FedACT将平均任务完成时间(JCT)降低最多8.3倍，模型准确率提升最多44.5%。

Conclusion: FedACT通过异构资源感知的跨任务设备调度，有效解决了多FL系统中的设备异构性和资源低效问题，显著提升了系统性能和模型精度。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative intelligence across decentralized data source devices in a privacy-preserving way. While substantial research attention has been drawn to optimizing the learning process for an individual task, real-world applications increasingly require multiple machine learning tasks simultaneously training their models across a shared pool of devices. Naively applying single-FL optimization techniques in multi-FL systems results in suboptimal system performance, particularly due to device heterogeneity and resource inefficiency. To address such a critical open challenge, we introduce {\em FedACT}, a novel resource heterogeneity-aware device scheduling approach designed to efficiently schedule heterogeneous devices across multiple concurrent FL jobs, with the goal of minimizing their average job completion time (JCT). {\em FedACT} dynamically assigns devices to FL jobs based on an alignment scoring mechanism that evaluates the compatibility between available resources of devices and resource demands of jobs. Additionally, it incorporates participation fairness to ensure balanced contributions from devices across jobs, further enhancing the accuracy levels of learned global models. An optimal scheduling plan is formulated in {\em FedACT} by prioritizing devices with higher alignment scores, while ensuring fair participation across jobs. To evaluate the effectiveness of the proposed scheduling algorithm, we carried out comprehensive experiments using diverse FL jobs and benchmark datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that {\em FedACT} reduces the average JCT by up to 8.3\(\times\) and improves model accuracy by up to 44.5\%, compared to the state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [76] [What Physics do Data-Driven MoCap-to-Radar Models Learn?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00018)
*Kevin Chen,Kenneth W. Parker,Anish Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套基于物理的可解释性框架，通过两个互补指标评估数据驱动的MoCap-to-radar模型是否真正学习了底层物理规律。实验发现，低重建误差并不保证物理一致性，部分模型误差低但物理指标表现差；对Transformer模型而言，时间注意力机制是学习物理规律的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的MoCap-to-radar模型能够生成合理的微多普勒频谱图，但尚不清楚这些模型是否真正学习了底层的物理原理，而非仅拟合数据表面模式。

Method: 提出两个互补的物理一致性指标：(1) 模型预测与物理推导的多普勒频率之间的对齐程度；(2) 在速度干预下，预测是否保持速度-频率关系。两个指标仅需MoCap输入和模型预测，无需实测雷达数据。

Result: 跨越多种模型架构的实验表明，低重建误差并不等同于物理一致性；部分模型误差低但物理指标得分差。对于Transformer模型，时间注意力机制是学习底层物理规律的关键因素。

Conclusion: 仅凭重建误差不足以评估模型对物理规律的学习程度，需要引入物理可解释性指标来甄别模型是否真正理解了背后的物理机制。

Abstract: Data-driven MoCap-to-radar models generate plausible micro-Doppler spectrograms, but do they actually learn the underlying physics? We introduce a physics-based interpretability framework to answer this question via two proposed complementary metrics: one measures alignment between model predictions and the physics-derived Doppler frequency, while the other tests whether predictions preserve the velocity-frequency relationship under velocity intervention. Both metrics require only MoCap input and model predictions, without access to measured radar data. Experiments across several model architectures reveal that low reconstruction error does not guarantee physical consistency: some, but not all, models achieve low error yet perform poorly on the two physics-based metrics. Further analysis shows that temporal attention is critical for transformer-based models to learn the underlying physics.

</details>


### [77] [AirFM-DDA: Air-Interface Foundation Model in the Delay-Doppler-Angle Domain for AI-Native 6G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00020)
*Kejia Bian,Meixia Tao,Jianhua Mo,Zhiyong Chen,Leyan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AirFM-DDA，一种在延迟-多普勒-角度（DDA）域运行的空中接口基础模型，通过将CSI从空时频域重参数化到DDA域以显式解析多径分量，并采用基于窗口的注意力机制和帧结构感知位置编码，在信道预测和估计任务上实现了优越的零样本泛化性能，同时将训练和推理成本降低近一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无线基础模型在空时频（STF）域处理信道状态信息（CSI），该域中不同多径分量天然叠加且结构纠缠，阻碍了通用信道表征的学习；同时，全局注意力机制带来了难以承受的计算开销。

Method: AirFM-DDA将CSI从STF域重参数化到延迟-多普勒-角度（DDA）域，显式沿物理意义明确的轴解析多径分量；采用基于窗口的注意力模块，配合帧结构感知位置编码（FS-PE），以对齐局部聚集的多径依赖关系并注入帧结构先验，避免二次复杂度的全局注意力。

Result: 在未见过的场景和数据集上实现优越的零样本泛化，在信道预测和估计任务中持续优于基线方法；基于窗口的注意力相比全局注意力将训练和推理成本降低近一个数量级；在高移动性、大延迟扩展、严重噪声和极端混叠条件下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DDA域重参数化结合窗口注意力机制是构建高效、泛化能力强的物理层无线基础模型的有效途径。

Abstract: The success of large foundation models is catalyzing a new paradigm for AI-native 6G network design: wireless foundation models for physical layer design. However, existing models often operate on channel state information (CSI) in the space-time-frequency (STF) domain, where distinct multipath components are inherently superimposed and structurally entangled. This hinders the learning of universal channel representation. Meanwhile, their reliance on global attention mechanisms incurs prohibitive computational overhead. In this paper, we propose AirFM-DDA, an Air-interface Foundation Model operating in the Delay-Doppler-Angle (DDA) domain for physicallayer tasks. Specifically, AirFM-DDA reparameterizes CSI from the STF domain into the DDA domain to explicitly resolve multipath components along physically meaningful axes. It employs a window-based attention module augmented with framestructure-aware positional encoding (FS-PE). This window-based attention aligns with locally clustered multipath dependencies while avoiding quadratic-complexity global attention, and FS-PE injects frame-structure priors into network. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AirFM-DDA achieves superior zero-shot generalization across unseen scenarios and datasets, consistently outperforming the baselines on channel prediction and estimation tasks. Compared to the global attention, its window-based attention reduces training and inference costs by nearly an order of magnitude. Moreover, AirFM-DDA maintains robustness under high mobility, large delay spreads, severe noise, and extreme aliasing conditions.

</details>


### [78] [Learning physically grounded traffic accident reconstruction from public accident reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00050)
*Yanchen Guan,Haicheng Liao,Chengyue Wang,Zhenning Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出从公开事故报告中自动重建交通事故场景的参数化多模态学习方法，构建了包含6,217个真实事故案例的CISS-REC数据集，开发了融合道路拓扑、参与者属性、预碰撞运动重建和碰撞交互优化的重建框架，在多项指标上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通事故记录以文本报告为主，但精确的物理事故重建因缺乏详细的场景测量数据和专家重建而难以规模化推广，现有方法成本高、数据稀缺。

Method: 将事故重建建模为参数化多模态学习问题；构建CISS-REC数据集（6,217个NHTSA真实事故案例）；提出重建框架，包含：报告语义与道路拓扑及参与者属性的对齐、车道一致预碰撞运动重建、基于局部几何推理和时间分配的碰撞交互优化。

Result: 在CISS-REC数据集上优于代表性基线方法，取得最佳整体重建保真度，包括更优的事故点精度和碰撞一致性。

Conclusion: 公开事故报告可作为可扩展的计算数据源用于定量可验证的事故重建，对交通安全分析、仿真和自动驾驶研究具有潜在价值。

Abstract: Traffic accidents are routinely documented in textual reports, yet physically grounded accident reconstruction remains difficult because detailed scene measurements and expert reconstructions are scarce, costly and hard to scale. Here we formulate accident reconstruction from publicly accessible reports and scene measurements as a parameterized multimodal learning problem. We construct CISS-REC, a dataset of 6,217 real-world accident cases curated from the NHTSA Crash Investigation Sampling System, and develop a reconstruction framework that grounds report semantics to road topology and participant attributes, reconstructs lane consistent pre-impact motion, and refines collision relevant interactions through localized geometric reasoning and temporal allocation. Our method outperforms representative baselines on CISS-REC, achieving the strongest overall reconstruction fidelity, including improved accident point accuracy and collision consistency. These results show that public accident reports can serve as scalable computational substrates for quantitatively verifiable accident reconstruction, with potential value for traffic safety analysis, simulation and autonomous driving research.

</details>


### [79] [Smart Ensemble Learning Framework for Predicting Groundwater Heavy Metal Pollution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00056)
*T. Ansah-Narh,G. Y. Afrifa,J. B. Tandoh,K. Asare,M. Addi,K. E. Yorke,D. M. A. Akpoley,K. Aidoo,S. K. Fosuhene*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究针对Densu盆地地下水重金属污染指数（HPI）的预测问题，提出了一种结合响应变量变换（原始、对数、高斯连接函数）与嵌套交叉验证集成机器学习（六种基学习器+Stacked Lasso集成）的框架。结果表明：原始尺度模型存在过度乐观（R²≈1.0）；对数变换稳定了方差（SVM: R²=0.93）；高斯连接函数表现最优（集成模型R²=0.96, RMSE=0.19），残差改善且空间图合理。DBSCAN聚类识别Fe和Mn为HPI主要贡献因子。局限在于非空间交叉验证和区域特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Densu盆地地下水重金属污染日益严重，但传统方法无法捕捉污染指标的统计复杂性和空间异质性。关键挑战在于HPI通常呈偏态分布且受相关污染物影响，未经变换会导致预测偏差。

Method: 构建了整合响应变量变换（原始、对数、高斯连接函数）与嵌套交叉验证集成机器学习的预测框架，使用六种基学习器（SVM、k-NN、CART、Elastic Net、核岭回归）和Stacked Lasso集成，并结合DBSCAN聚类进行贡献因子分析。

Result: 原始尺度模型R²≈1.0（过度乐观）；对数变换SVM R²=0.93；高斯连接函数集成模型R²=0.96, RMSE=0.19；DBSCAN识别Fe和Mn为主要贡献因子。

Conclusion: 分布感知的集成学习结合聚类诊断能够为地下水污染评估提供稳健且可解释的框架；未来需引入空间验证并拓展至其他地质环境。

Abstract: Groundwater in the Densu Basin is increasingly threatened by heavy metal contamination, but conventional methods fail to capture the statistical complexity and spatial heterogeneity of pollution indicators. A key challenge is modelling the Heavy Metal Pollution Index (HPI), which is typically skewed and affected by correlated contaminants, leading to biased predictions without transformation. This study develops a predictive framework integrating response transformations with nested cross-validated ensemble machine learning. Three transformations (raw, log, and Gaussian copula) were applied to HPI and evaluated across six learners: support vector regression (SVM), $k$-nearest neighbours (k-NN), CART, Elastic Net, kernel ridge regression, and a stacked Lasso ensemble. Raw-scale models produced deceptively high fits (Elastic Net and stacked ensemble $R^2 \approx 1.0$), suggesting over-optimism. The log transformation stabilised variance (SVM: $R^2 = 0.93$, RMSE $= 0.18$; k-NN: $R^2 = 0.92$, RMSE $= 0.20$). The Gaussian copula gave the most reliable results: stacked ensemble $R^2 = 0.96$ (RMSE $= 0.19$), with other learners maintaining high accuracy. Copula-based models improved residuals and produced spatially plausible maps. DBSCAN clustering revealed Fe and Mn as primary HPI contributors, consistent with regional hydrogeochemistry. Limitations include reliance on random (not spatial) cross-validation and basin-specific scope. Future work should explore spatial validation and other geological settings. Overall, distribution-aware ensembles with clustering diagnostics offer robust, interpretable assessments of groundwater contamination.

</details>


### [80] [CRADIPOR: Crash Dispersion Predictor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00070)
*Edgar Chaillou,Sebastian Rodriguez,Yves Tourbier,Francisco Chinesta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CRADIPOR，一种基于秩缩减自编码器（RRAE）和监督分类的数值色散预测工具，可在不重复计算的情况下，于汽车碰撞仿真后处理中识别对数值色散敏感的区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车碰撞有限元仿真因并行计算和模型复杂性导致结果不可严格重复，后处理中的性能指标存在显著数值色散，影响工程决策；而通过重复仿真估计色散的计算成本过高，不切实际。

Method: 采用秩缩减自编码器（RRAE）结合监督分类，在不重复计算的前提下，于常规碰撞仿真后处理流程中识别对数值色散敏感的区域。

Result: RRAE框架在数据集上优于随机森林基线；小波基和斜率基信号表示效果最佳，其中斜率变化分类性能最优。

Conclusion: 结构化潜在表示有助于提升汽车碰撞后处理中数值色散检测的效果。

Abstract: We present CRADIPOR, a numerical dispersion prediction tool for automotive crash simulations. Finite Element (FE) crash models are widely used throughout vehicle development, but their predictions are not strictly repeatable because of parallel computation and model complexity. As a result, performance criteria evaluated during post-processing may exhibit significant numerical dispersion, which complicates engineering decision-making. Although dispersion can be estimated by repeating the same simulation, this approach is generally impractical because of its high computational cost.
  This work therefore investigates a prediction tool that can be applied during routine crash-simulation post-processing without repeating the computation. The proposed approach relies on a Rank Reduction Autoencoder (RRAE) combined with supervised classification in order to identify regions sensitive to numerical dispersion. The comparative analysis suggests that the RRAE-based framework is more effective than the Random Forest baseline on the studied dataset. Among the tested signal representations, wavelet-based and slope-based inputs appear to be the most promising, with slope variations providing the best classification performance. These results support the use of structured latent representations for improving numerical-dispersion detection in automotive crash post-processing.

</details>


### [81] [Hyperspherical Forward-Forward with Prototypical Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00082)
*Shalini Sarode,Brian Moser,Joachim Folz,Federico Raue,Tobias Nauen,Stanislav Frolov,Andreas Dengel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出超球前向-前向算法(HFF)，将每层局部目标从二值拟合重构为超球特征空间中的多类分类问题，通过学习类别原型锚点实现单次前向传播推理和权重更新，相比原始FF算法提速40倍以上，并在ImageNet-1k上首次实现超过25%的top-1准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始前向-前向(FF)算法在训练时高效，但推理过程需要为每个类别单独进行前向传播，计算成本极高，成为其实际应用的主要瓶颈。

Method: 将每层局部学习目标从二值"拟合优度"任务重构为超球特征空间中的直接多类分类问题；学习一组类别特定的单位范数原型作为几何锚点和隐式负样本，使权重更新和推理可在单次前向传播中完成。

Result: 推理速度比原始FF算法快40倍以上；作为贪心局部学习方法首次在ImageNet-1k上报告超过25%的top-1准确率；迁移学习达到65.96%的top-1准确率。

Conclusion: HFF算法简单易实现，能有效扩展到现代卷积架构，在图像分类基准上实现了与反向传播相接近的准确率，弥合了局部学习与反向传播之间的性能差距。

Abstract: The Forward-Forward (FF) algorithm presents a compelling, bio-inspired alternative to backpropagation. However, while efficient in training, it has a computationally prohibitive inference process that requires a separate forward pass for every class that is evaluated. In this work, we introduce the Hyperspherical Forward-Forward (HFF), a novel reformulation that resolves this critical bottleneck. Our core innovation is to reframe the local objective of each layer from a binary goodness-of-fit task to a direct multi-class classification problem within a hyperspherical feature space. We achieve this by learning a set of class-specific, unit-norm prototypes that act as geometric anchors and implicit negatives. This architectural innovation preserves the benefits of local training while enabling weight update and inference in a single forward pass, making it >40x faster than the original FF algorithm. Our method is simple to implement, scales effectively to modern convolutional architectures, and achieves superior accuracy on standard image classification benchmarks, closing the gap with backpropagation. Most notably, we are among the first greedy local-learning methods to report over 25% top-1 accuracy on ImageNet-1k, and 65.96% with transfer learning.

</details>


### [82] [Comparative Analysis of Polygon-Based and Global Machine Learning Models for Bus Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00083)
*Daniel Azenkot,Michael Fire,Eran Ben Elia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合空间聚类与多维特征分析的公交客流量预测框架，通过将城市划分为不同区域并为每个区域训练本地预测模型，实现了与全局模型相当的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预测模型将整个城市视为单一同质区域，无法捕捉不同城市区域的独特和局部动态，导致预测精度受限。

Method: 利用公交客流量数据（按线路、时间、站点）及开源数据（空间特征、气象条件、时间模式），基于邻近站点共享相似客流量特征的原理对城市进行空间聚类，为每个聚类训练独立的本地预测模型。

Result: 本地化预测方法达到了与全局模型相当的预测精度。

Conclusion: 空间感知的本地化建模策略对公共交通预测是有效的，为实现更具针对性和高效的公交服务改进铺平了道路。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of bus ridership (passengers numbers) is crucial for efficient management and optimization of public transport systems. Traditional forecasting models often fail to capture the unique and localized dynamics of different urban areas by treating the entire city as a single, homogeneous region. This paper introduces a novel framework that enhances bus ridership prediction by integrating a spatial clustering methodology with multi-dimensional feature analysis. The proposed framework utilizes a diverse set of data, including bus ridership data (by route number, time, and bus stop) complemented by a variety of open source data, such as spatial features (e.g., attractive destinations), meteorological conditions (e.g., temperature, rainfall), and temporal patterns (e.g., time of day, day of week). By clustering the urban area into distinct regions, based on the principle that bus stops in close proximity share similar ridership characteristics, a separate local forecasting model is trained for each of these clusters. This localized approach demonstrates an accuracy comparable to that of global models. The findings suggest that a spatially-aware, localized modeling strategy is effective for public transport prediction, paving the way for more targeted and efficient service improvements.

</details>


### [83] [SPLICE: Latent Diffusion over JEPA Embeddings for Conformal Time-Series Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00126)
*Arnaud Zinflou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPLICE是一个将潜在生成式插补与无分布、自适应在线预测区间相结合的时间序列插补框架，在13个电力负荷数据集上实现了最先进的插补精度和覆盖保证，并展示了强大的跨域迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式时间序列插补模型虽然重构精度高，但缺乏有限样本可靠性保证，这在电力系统中插补值用于调度和规划时是一个关键限制。

Method: SPLICE框架包含四个核心组件：JEPA编码器将日负荷段映射到64维潜在空间；条件潜在桥接（含四种采样模式）生成候选间隙轨迹；逐小时条件解码器映射回信号空间；自适应共形推断（ACI）提供具有覆盖保证的预测带。其中流匹配变体在5-10步ODE中达到与DDIM相当的质量，速度提升5-10倍。

Result: 在13个负荷数据集（9个专有、3个UCI Electricity、ETTh1）上，SPLICE在91天间隙设置下赢得9/12个非退化数据集的最低Load-only MSE（0.056），在所有间隙长度下赢得18/32个；CRPS最佳（0.161，比最强竞品低18.3%）；ACI实现93-95%经验覆盖，纠正了静态共形预测高达7.5个百分点的覆盖不足；池化JEPA编码器训练的模型可通过快速桥接微调迁移到4个未见领域。

Conclusion: SPLICE通过将自监督潜在插补与自适应共形预测结合，同时实现了高精度插补和严格有限样本覆盖保证，在电力系统时间序列插补任务中全面超越现有基线，并具备良好的跨域迁移能力。

Abstract: Generative models for time-series imputation achieve strong reconstruction accuracy, yet provide no finite-sample reliability guarantees, a critical limitation in power systems where imputed values inform dispatch and planning. We introduce SPLICE (Self-supervised Predictive Latent Inpainting with Conformal Envelopes), a modular framework coupling latent generative imputation with distribution-free, online-adaptive prediction intervals. A JEPA encoder maps daily load segments into a 64-dimensional latent space; a conditional latent bridge with four sampling modes generates candidate gap trajectories; an hourly-conditioned decoder maps back to signal space; and Adaptive Conformal Inference (ACI) wraps the output with coverage-guaranteed prediction bands. The flow-matching variant achieves comparable quality to DDIM in 5--10 ODE steps (5-10x speedup). On thirteen load datasets (nine proprietary, three UCI Electricity, ETTh1), SPLICE achieves the lowest mean Load-only MSE (0.056), winning 9/12 non-degenerate datasets at 91-day gaps and 18/32 across all gap lengths vs. five established baselines, and produces the best CRPS (0.161, -18.3% vs. the strongest competitor). ACI delivers 93--95% empirical coverage, correcting under-coverage failures of up to 7.5 pp observed with static conformal prediction. A pooled JEPA encoder trained on nine feeds transfers to four unseen domains, matching or exceeding per-dataset oracles with only a quick bridge fine-tuning.

</details>


### [84] [Learning Fingerprints for Medical Time Series with Redundancy-Constrained Information Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00130)
*Huayu Li,ZhengXiao He,Xiwen Chen,Jingjing Wang,Siyuan Tian,Jinghao Wen,Ao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于交叉注意力瓶颈和双目标优化的框架，将可变长度医疗时间序列压缩为k个离散的指纹令牌，并通过率失真与多样性惩罚实现解缠表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督方法（如MAE）虽能预训练通用编码器，但无法显式学习紧凑且语义可解释的潜在表示，通常依赖全局平均池化或[CLS]令牌等启发式聚合策略，导致表示冗余且缺乏可解释性。

Method: 1) 使用交叉注意力瓶颈将可变长度MedTS压缩为固定大小的k个指纹令牌；2) 双目标优化：重构损失确保令牌是原始数据的充分统计量，基于总编码率（TCR）的多样性惩罚最小化令牌间冗余；3) 理论框架：解缠率失真问题。

Result: 产生低维、可解释且样本高效的表示，每个令牌被鼓励捕获独立的变异因子，为鲁棒的数字化生物标志物奠定基础。

Conclusion: 该方法通过显式解缠机制克服了现有自监督方法在医疗时间序列表示学习中可解释性不足的问题，有望提升下游任务性能与数字生物标志物的可靠性。

Abstract: Learning meaningful representations from medical time series (MedTS) such as ECG or EEG signals is a critical challenge. These signals are often high-dimensional, variable-length and rife with noise. Existing self-supervised approaches, such as Masked Autoencoders (MAEs) are highly effective for pre-training general-purpose encoders. However, they do not explicitly learn compact and semantically interpretable latent representations, typically relying on heuristic aggregation strategies such as global average pooling or a designated [CLS] token. We propose a novel framework that compresses a variable-length MedTS into a fixed-size set of $k$ latent Fingerprint Tokens. Our architecture employs a cross-attention bottleneck to generate these tokens and is trained with a dual-objective function. The first objective is a reconstruction loss, which ensures the tokens are \textit{sufficient statistics} for the original data. The second, a diversity penalty based on the Total Coding Rate (TCR), explicitly minimizes the redundancy between tokens, encouraging them to become statistically \textit{disentangled} representations. We present the theoretical justification for our method, framing it as a novel \textbf{Disentangled Rate-Distortion} problem. This approach produces a low-dimensional, interpretable, and sample-efficient representation, where each token is encouraged to capture an independent factor of variation, paving the way for more robust digital biomarkers.

</details>


### [85] [Smart Profit-Aware Crop Advisory System: Kisan AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00133)
*Debasis Dwibedy,Avyay Nishtala,Pranathi Mukku,D Snehaja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Kisan AI 是一个智能利润感知作物咨询系统，通过在标准农业特征中引入市场价格变量，利用随机森林模型（99.3%准确率）进行作物推荐，并集成价格预测、病害检测和多语言AI聊天机器人功能，解决传统系统忽视经济效益的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有作物咨询系统存在"经济盲目性"：仅优化生物产量而忽略市场价格，导致农民做出农学上合理但经济上不可行的决策。

Method: 在标准七个农学特征基础上增加市场价格变量构成九特征数据集；训练随机森林分类器并与八个基线模型对比；集成Facebook Prophet进行六个月价格预测、MobileNetV2病害检测模块、基于Anthropic Claude API的九语言AI聊天机器人，部署为渐进式Web应用。

Result: 随机森林模型在九特征数据集上达到99.3%的最高准确率和最低对数损失，验证了市场价格作为预测特征的有效性和影响力。

Conclusion: 通过将市场价格纳入预测特征并构建全栈应用，Kisan AI能够为印度农民提供经济上可行的作物推荐，弥合农学最优与经济最优之间的差距。

Abstract: Modern crop advisory systems exhibit a critical limitation termed \textit{economic blindness}. These systems primarily optimize for biological yield, often overlooking market price, which can lead farmers toward agronomically sound yet financially unviable decisions. In this paper, we develop Kisan AI, a smart profit-aware crop advisory system that resolves the above-mentioned limitation through a research-driven, full-stack application. We train the Random Forest(RF) classifier model on a nine-feature benchmark dataset, the standard seven agronomic attributes augmented with a \textit{market\_price} variable, and evaluated against eight baseline models, considering the evaluation matrices, such as, accuracy, precision, recall, F1-score, and Log Loss. The RF model achieves the highest accuracy of 99.3\% and the lowest Log Loss, confirming that the inclusion of market price as a predictive feature is both valid and impactful. We then implement the RF model within a multilingual progressive Web App alongside a Facebook Prophet six-month price forecasting engine and a MobileNetV2 disease detection module. A nine-language AI chatbot powered by the Anthropic Claude API unifies all modules into a single, mobile-installable platform accessible to farmers across India.

</details>


### [86] [Technical Report: Activation Residual Hessian Quantization (ARHQ) for Low-Bit LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00140)
*YiFeng Wang,Zhun Sun,Keisuke Sakaguchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ARHQ提出了一种基于激活残差Hessian的后训练权重重分裂方法，通过构造输入侧残差Hessian矩阵识别误差敏感权重方向，并将其分离到高精度低秩分支中，从而缓解低比特激活-权重量化中的误差传播问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低比特激活-权重量化方法（如W4A4、W4A8等）在极端量化条件下存在严重的误差传播问题，导致模型推理性能显著下降。尤其是在激活值也被量化时，量化误差会逐层累积放大。

Method: 1) 从激活量化残差G_x构建输入侧残差Hessian矩阵；2) 对缩放后的权重矩阵W G^{1/2}_x进行截断SVD分解；3) 将误差敏感的方向分离到高精度低秩分支中，同时其余部分保持低比特量化。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507模型上，ARHQ显著提升了逐层信噪比(SNR)，并在ZebraLogic推理任务上即使在激进量化条件下仍能保持下游推理性能。

Conclusion: ARHQ通过理论驱动的残差Hessian分析和低秩分支分离，有效缓解了激活-权重量化中的误差传播问题，为大规模语言模型在资源受限场景下的部署提供了可行的量化方案。

Abstract: We present Activation Residual Hessian Quantization (ARHQ), a post-training weight splitting method designed to mitigate error propagation in low-bit activation-weight quantization. By constructing an input-side residual Hessian from activation quantization residuals (G_x), ARHQ analytically identifies and isolates error-sensitive weight directions into a high-precision low-rank branch. This is achieved via a closed-form truncated SVD on the scaled weight matrix W G^{1/2}_x . Experimental results on Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507 demonstrate that ARHQ significantly improves layer-wise SNR and preserves downstream reasoning performance on ZebraLogic even under aggressive quantization. The code is available at https://github.com/BeautMoonQ/ARHQ.

</details>


### [87] [Consistent Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00161)
*Hasan Amin,Yuan Gao,Yaser Souri,Subhojit Som,Ming Yin,Rajiv Khanna,Xia Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多路径离散一致性（MPDC）原则，并实例化为一致性扩散语言模型（CDLM），通过训练去噪器在随机后验桥上的路径不变性，实现了单阶段、无教师训练的高质量离散文本生成，在少步采样场景下显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型虽有望实现亚线性并行生成，但高质量样本仍需大量精炼步骤。连续域中的一致性训练依赖于概率流ODE，而离散扩散不存在样本空间ODE，无法直接适配。因此需要为离散扩散设计一种替代的一致性训练框架。

Method: 提出多路径离散一致性（MPDC）原则，利用精确后验桥（而非确定性轨迹）训练去噪器在随机桥上的路径不变性（期望意义下）。实例化为一致扩散语言模型（CDLM），是一个单阶段、无教师训练的统一框架，将掩码扩散、连续一致性模型和渐进/离散蒸馏统一为同一视角下的解析极限或经验近似。

Result: CDLM在条件和非条件文本生成任务上均达到新SOTA，一致超越强基线的离散扩散模型，甚至超越多阶段蒸馏基线，在少步采样场景下增益最大。

Conclusion: CDLM为下一代快速、高保真离散生成建模提供了有原则且可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) are an attractive alternative to autoregressive models because they promise sublinear-time, parallel generation, yet practical gains remain elusive as high-quality samples still demand hundreds of refinement steps. In continuous domains, consistency training along the probability-flow ODE is a popular recipe to accelerate diffusion. For discrete diffusion, no analogous sample-space ODE exists, making direct adaptation ill-defined. We argue that the natural discrete substitute is not a deterministic trajectory but its stochastic counterpart: the exact posterior bridge, available in closed form for broad corruption families including masked and uniform diffusion. Building on this observation, we introduce Multi-Path Discrete Consistency (MPDC), a new principle that trains a denoiser to be path-invariant in expectation across these stochastic bridges, and instantiate it as the Consistent Diffusion Language Model (CDLM), a single-stage, teacher-free training framework. A single CDLM objective unifies masked diffusion, continuous consistency models, and progressive/discrete distillation as analytic limits or empirical approximations of one common view. Empirically, CDLM establishes a new state of the art on both conditional and unconditional text-generation, consistently outperforming strong base discrete diffusion models and often even multi-stage distilled baselines across sampling budgets, with the largest gains in the few-step regime. Together, these results position CDLM as a principled and scalable foundation for the next generation of fast, high-fidelity discrete generative modeling.

</details>


### [88] [Towards A Generative Protein Evolution Machine with DPLM-Evo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00182)
*Xinyou Wang,Liang Hong,Jiasheng Ye,Zaixiang Zheng,Yu Li,Shujian Huang,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DPLM-Evo，一种进化离散扩散框架，通过显式预测替换、插入和删除操作来模拟蛋白质的逐步进化，在序列理解、突变效应预测和可变长度生成任务上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DPLM依赖基于掩码的吸收扩散，这与蛋白质通过累计编辑而非从掩码中涌现的生物学直觉相悖，缺乏对替换和插入/删除操作的显式预训练目标，限制了优化式后编辑和灵活引导生成。

Method: 提出DPLM-Evo框架：1) 将上采样长度的潜在对齐空间与可变长度观测序列空间解耦，实现可处理的indel感知生成；2) 引入上下文感知的进化噪声核，产生生物学信息丰富的上下文依赖突变模式；3) 在去噪过程中显式预测替换、插入和删除三种操作。

Result: 在序列理解任务上表现优异，在单序列设置下ProteinGym突变效应预测达到最先进水平；支持可变长度模拟进化和通过显式编辑轨迹对现有蛋白质进行后编辑/优化。

Conclusion: DPLM-Evo通过将扩散过程与真实的生物学进化机制对齐，有效弥合了生成式蛋白质语言模型与进化生物学之间的差距，为蛋白质理解和生成提供了更符合自然规律的框架。

Abstract: Proteins are shaped by gradual evolution under biophysical and functional constraints. Protein language models learn rich evolutionary constraints from large-scale sequences, and discrete diffusion-based protein language models~(\eg, DPLMs) are promising for both understanding and generation. However, existing DPLMs typically rely on masking-based absorbing diffusion that contradicts a simple biological intuition: proteins evolve through accumulated edits, not by emerging from masks. Consequently, these frameworks lack explicit pretraining objectives for substitution and insertion/deletion (indel) operations, limiting both optimization-style post-editing and flexible guided generation. To address these limitations, we present DPLM-Evo, an evolutionary discrete diffusion framework that explicitly predicts substitution, insertion, and deletion operations during denoising. DPLM-Evo decouples an upsampled-length latent alignment space from the variable-length observed sequence space, which makes indel-aware generation tractable and enables adaptive scaffold growth throughout the process with negligible computational overhead. To better align substitutions with real evolution, we further introduce a contextualized evolutionary noising kernel that produces biologically informed, context-dependent mutation patterns. Across tasks, DPLM-Evo improves sequence understanding and achieves state-of-the-art mutation effect prediction performance on ProteinGym in the single-sequence setting. It also enables variable-length simulated evolution, and post-editing/optimization of existing proteins via explicit edit trajectories.

</details>


### [89] [Introducing WARM-VR: Benchmark Dataset for Multimodal Wearable Affect Recognition in Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00184)
*Karim Alghoul,Faisal Mohd,Fedwa Laamarti,Hussein Al Osman,Abdulmotaleb El Saddik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了WARM-VR，一个面向沉浸式多感官虚拟现实环境的情感识别多模态公开数据集。该数据集包含31名受试者在VR放松任务（沙滩场景+嗅觉增强）与压力诱导任务（算术题）中的穿戴传感器数据（手腕BVP/EDA/温度/加速度、胸部ECG），并基于自我报告问卷和生理信号分析验证了VR放松（尤其嗅觉增强）能显著降低负面情绪。基准测试显示，CNN和CNN-Bi-GRU在效价二分类上F1达0.63/AUC 0.69，轻量级Transformer在唤醒度上表现最均衡，CNN-Bi-GRU在放松任务中F1达0.64/AUC 0.69。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感识别数据集大多基于静态环境，难以适用于虚拟现实（VR）等沉浸式多媒体场景。为填补这一空白，作者旨在构建一个支持沉浸式、多感官环境中情感识别的公开多模态数据集。

Method: 1) 数据采集：31名受试者（19-37岁），佩戴手腕传感器（BVP、EDA、皮肤温度、三轴加速度）和胸部ECG传感器；2) 实验范式：先通过算术任务诱导压力，再通过VR沙滩场景（含视觉、听觉、嗅觉同步刺激）诱导放松；3) 情感标注：主观采用验证过的自我报告问卷，客观分析生理测量值；4) 基准测试：采用CNN、CNN-Bi-GRU、轻量级Transformer等机器学习算法进行效价/唤醒度二分类及放松任务分类。

Result: 统计验证VR放松（尤其嗅觉增强）能显著降低负面情绪。效价二分类：CNN和CNN-Bi-GRU最佳（F1=0.63, AUC=0.69）；唤醒度二分类：轻量级Transformer最均衡（F1-0=0.54, F1-1=0.63）；放松任务：CNN-Bi-GRU最优（F1=0.64, AUC=0.69）。

Conclusion: WARM-VR数据集为沉浸式VR环境中的情感识别研究提供了有价值的公开资源，验证了多感官刺激（特别是嗅觉增强）对放松效果的积极作用，并为后续研究建立了可参照的机器学习基线。

Abstract: With the growing integration of human-computer interaction into everyday life, advances in machine learning have enabled systems to better perceive and respond to users' emotional states. Most existing affect recognition datasets focus on static environments, limiting their applicability to immersive multimedia contexts such as Virtual Reality (VR). In this paper, we introduce WARM-VR, a novel publicly available multimodal dataset designed to support affect recognition in immersive, multisensory environments using wearable sensing instrumentation. Data were collected from 31 participants aged 19-37 using wearable sensors: a wristband measuring Blood Volume Pulse (BVP), EDA, skin Temperature, three-axis Acceleration, and a chest strap recording ECG signals. Participants engaged in immersive VR experiences designed to elicit relaxation through a calming beach environment following stress induction via an arithmetic task. These sessions incorporated synchronized multimedia stimuli: visual, auditory, and olfactory. Affective states were assessed subjectively through validated self-report questionnaires and objectively through the analysis of physiological measurements. Statistical analysis of the questionnaires confirmed that VR relaxation significantly reduced negative affect, particularly with olfactory enhancement. Furthermore, we established a benchmark on the dataset using widely recognized machine learning algorithms. The best performance for binary classification from BVP data of valence, was obtained with a CNN and a CNN-Bi-GRU model, both achieving an average F1-score of 0.63 and an AUC of 0.69. For arousal, a lightweight Transformer architecture provided the most balanced results (F1-0 0.54 and F1-1 0.63), outperforming recurrent hybrids. In the relaxation task, a CNN-Bi-GRU model reached the highest overall performance (average F1-score 0.64, AUC 0.69).

</details>


### [90] [Fair Dataset Distillation via Cross-Group Barycenter Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00185)
*Mohammad Hossein Moslemi,Nima Hosseini Dashtbayaz,Zhimin Mei,Boyu Wang,Bissan Ghaddar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 数据集蒸馏在压缩数据时会导致不同人口统计子组的性能差异，产生公平性差距。本文提出通过蒸馏到群体不平衡无关的预测信息重心来减少这种偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集蒸馏方法在压缩数据时忽略了不同人口统计子组之间的预测模式差异，导致某些子组性能显著下降，产生公平性问题。本文旨在分析和解决这一公平性差距。

Method: 首先形式化分析了群体不平衡和预测模式差异两种偏差源的相互作用，提出将问题转化为寻找群体不平衡无关的预测信息重心（barycenter），通过蒸馏到共享的聚合表示来促使所有子组产生相似的表征。

Result: 该方法与现有蒸馏方法兼容，且实证结果表明能显著减少数据集蒸馏引入的偏差。

Conclusion: 仅纠正群体不平衡无法消除公平性差距，必须从预测模式匹配角度出发。通过蒸馏到群体无关的重心表示可以有效缓解数据集蒸馏中的公平性问题。

Abstract: Dataset Distillation aims to compress a large dataset into a small synthetic one while maintaining predictive performance. We show that as different demographic groups exhibit distinct predictive patterns, the distillation process struggles to simultaneously preserve informative signals for all subgroups, regardless of whether group sizes are mildly or severely imbalanced. Consequently, models trained on distilled data can experience substantial performance drops for certain subgroups, leading to fairness gaps. Crucially, these gaps do not disappear by merely correcting group imbalance, since they stem from fundamental mismatches in subgroup predictive patterns rather than from sample-size disparities alone. We therefore formally analyze the interaction between these two sources of bias and cast the solution as identifying a group-imbalance-agnostic barycenter of the predictive information that induces similar representations across all subgroups. By distilling toward this shared aggregate representation, we show that group fairness concerns can be reduced. Our approach is compatible with existing distillation methods, and empirical results show that it substantially reduces bias introduced by dataset distillation.

</details>


### [91] [High-Probability Convergence in Decentralized Stochastic Optimization with Gradient Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00281)
*Aleksandar Armacki,Haoyuan Cai,Ali H. Sayed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了去中心化随机优化中带梯度跟踪的DSGD算法（GT-DSGD）的高概率收敛性，在非凸和Polyak-Łojasiewicz成本函数下分别达到$\mathcal{O}(\log(1/\delta)/\sqrt{nT})$和$\mathcal{O}(\log(1/\delta)/nT)$的阶最优收敛率，首次为去中心化优化方法中融入偏差校正技术提供了高概率保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去中心化随机优化中的高概率收敛结果几乎仅针对DSGD算法，且需要强假设（如数据异质性有界、成本强凸），这远落后于均方误差意义下的结果——后者中融入偏差校正的方法能在更宽松假设下收敛且实践性能更优。本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 将梯度跟踪（gradient tracking）技术融入DSGD，提出GT-DSGD算法；在满足松弛子高斯条件的噪声假设下，利用截断技术等工具建立高概率收敛界。

Result: 非凸成本：$\mathcal{O}(\log(1/\delta)/\sqrt{nT})$；Polyak-Łojasiewicz成本：$\mathcal{O}(\log(1/\delta)/nT)$。这些收敛率在阶上最优，且与均方误差意义下的收敛条件一致。

Conclusion: GT-DSGD在高概率意义下收敛条件与均方误差意义下相同，达到可比暂态时间；这是首个为融入偏差校正的去中心化优化方法提供高概率保证的工作，实验验证了理论与实际优越性。

Abstract: We study high-probability (HP) convergence guarantees in decentralized stochastic optimization, where multiple agents collaborate to jointly train a model over a network. Existing HP results in decentralized settings almost exclusively focus on the Decentralized Stochastic Gradient Descent ($\mathtt{DSGD}$) algorithm, which requires strong assumptions, such as bounded data heterogeneity, or strong convexity of each agent's cost. This is contrary to the mean-squared error (MSE) results, where methods incorporating bias-correction techniques are known to converge under relaxed assumptions and achieve better practical performance. In this paper we provide the first step toward bridging the gap, by studying HP convergence of $\mathtt{DSGD}$ incorporating the gradient tracking technique, in the presence of noise satisfying a relaxed sub-Gaussian condition. We show that the resulting method, dubbed $\mathtt{GT-DSGD}$, achieves order-optimal HP convergence rates for both non-convex and Polyak-Łojasiewicz costs, of order $\mathcal{O}\Big(\frac{\log(1/δ)}{\sqrt{nT}}\Big)$ and $\mathcal{O}\Big(\frac{\log(1/δ)}{nT}\Big)$, respectively, where $n$ is the number of agents, $T$ is the time horizon and $δ\in (0,1)$ is the confidence parameter. Our results establish that $\mathtt{GT-DSGD}$ converges in the HP sense under the same conditions on the cost as in the MSE sense, while achieving comparable transient times. To the best of our knowledge, these are the first HP guarantees for decentralized optimization methods incorporating bias-correction. Numerical experiments on real and synthetic data verify our theoretical findings, underlining the superior performance of $\mathtt{GT-DSGD}$ and highlighting that the benefits of incorporating bias-correction are also maintained in the HP sense.

</details>


### [92] [Diversity in Large Language Models under Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00195)
*Roman Klypa,Oleksandr Cherednichenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TOFU损失函数，解决监督微调（SFT）导致的大语言模型生成多样性下降问题，该损失通过同时处理微调数据中低频模式被忽略和预训练知识遗忘两个原因，在保持响应质量的同时提升输出多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SFT虽然对对齐LLM至关重要，但被认为会抑制生成多样性。尽管这一现象频繁被提及，但缺乏正式的实证检验，且现有方法对此问题的分析视角各异，需要更深入的分析以取得进一步改进。

Method: 通过理论分析将多样性下降归因于两个主要因素：微调数据集中低频模式被忽略以及预训练知识的遗忘。基于此提出Tempered Focal (TOFU) loss，一种同时解决上述两个挑战的新型损失函数。

Result: 大规模实验证实SFT后生成广度确实缩小，验证了该假设。在多个模型和基准上，TOFU在保持高响应质量的同时提升了输出多样性。

Conclusion: TOFU为SFT提供了一种有原则的方法，在保持响应质量的前提下有效增强生成多样性，是对SFT领域的重要贡献。

Abstract: Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) is essential for aligning Large Language Models (LLMs) with user intent, yet it is believed to suppress generative diversity. Although this reduction is frequently referenced, formal empirical testing of the phenomenon remains limited. The expressiveness of LLMs by itself was addressed by multiple prior methods. Their varying perspectives suggest that deeper analysis could yield further improvements. In this study, we attribute the decline to two primary drivers: the neglect of low-frequency patterns within fine-tuning datasets and the forgetting of preexisting knowledge. Motivated by our theoretical analysis, we develop Tempered Focal (TOFU) loss, a novel objective that addresses both stated challenges simultaneously. Our extensive evaluation confirms at scale that generation breadth narrows after SFT and strengthens the hypothesis explaining this effect. Across multiple models and benchmarks, we demonstrate that TOFU enhances output diversity while preserving high response quality, offering a principled approach to SFT.

</details>


### [93] [Near-optimal and Efficient First-Order Algorithm for Multi-Task Learning with Shared Linear Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00473)
*Shihong Ding,Fangyu Du,Cong Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于似然的、可高效求解的一阶算法用于多任务学习，在共享线性表示场景下实现了$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1)$迭代收敛和$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(dk/(TN))$的近优估计误差，相较现有似然方法提升了$k$倍，证明了基于似然的一阶方法能够高效求解MTL问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务学习在经验上已取得显著成功，但基于似然的高效可解算法（即使是共享线性表示场景）仍发展不足，主要受限于矩阵分解中固有的非凸结构。

Method: 提出了一种一阶算法，联合学习共享表示和任务特定参数，并给出理论保证：$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1)$次迭代收敛，估计误差达$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(dk/(TN))$，相比现有基于似然的方法有$k$倍提升。

Result: 算法在$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1)$次迭代内收敛，达到近优估计误差$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(dk/(TN))$，优于现有似然方法$k$倍。

Conclusion: 研究结果证明，基于似然的一阶方法能够高效求解多任务学习问题，为后续研究提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Multi-task learning (MTL) has emerged as a pivotal paradigm in machine learning by leveraging shared structures across multiple related tasks. Despite its empirical success, the development of likelihood-based efficiently solvable algorithms--even for shared linear representations--remains largely underdeveloped, primarily due to the non-convex structure intrinsic to matrix factorization. This paper introduces a first-order algorithm that jointly learns a shared representation and task-specific parameters, with guaranteed efficiency. Notably, it converges in $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1)$ iterations and attains a \emph{near-optimal} estimation error of $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(dk/(TN))$, \emph{improving} over existing likelihood-based methods by a factor of $k$, where $d$, $k$, $T$, $N$ denote input dimension, representation dimension, task count, and samples per task, respectively. Our results justify that likelihood-based first-order methods can efficiently solve the MTL problem.

</details>


### [94] [CompleteRXN: Toward Completing Open Chemical Reaction Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00222)
*Gabriel Vogel,Minouk Noordsij,Evgeny Pidko,Jana M. Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CompleteRXN基准，用于评估化学反应补全方法；提出的CRB模型在随机划分上达99.20%准确率，在极端分布外划分上达91.12%，但所有方法在未 curated 的完整USPTO数据上性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有化学反应数据集（如USPTO）严重不完整，常缺失副产物、共反应物和化学计量系数，限制了其在下游应用中的可靠性和有效性。

Method: 构建了由对齐的不完整反应与原子平衡反应组成的CompleteRXN基准数据集；提出了约束反应平衡器（CRB），一种带约束解码的编码器-解码器反应补全模型；并与SynRBL等基线方法进行比较。

Result: CRB在随机划分上达99.20%等价准确率，在极端分布外划分上达91.12%；SynRBL生成许多平衡且化学合理的补全但准确率较低；所有方法在不完整度增加时性能下降，在完整未 curated 的USPTO数据上表现大幅下滑。

Conclusion: CompleteRXN基准有效评估了反应补全方法；CRB表现优异但存在基准性能与实际鲁棒性之间的差距，未来需进一步提升模型在真实场景下的实用性。

Abstract: Chemical reaction datasets such as USPTO suffer from substantial incompleteness, frequently missing byproducts, co-reactants, and stoichiometric coefficients. This limits their applicability and reliability in downstream applications. Here, we introduce CompleteRXN, a large-scale supervised benchmark for reaction completion under realistic missing-data conditions. We construct a dataset of aligned incomplete and atom-balanced reactions by mapping USPTO records to curated mechanistic reactions. We evaluate representative baselines, including a novel encoder-decoder reaction completion model with constrained decoding, the Constrained Reaction Balancer (CRB), and a recent algorithmic method, SynRBL. On our CompleteRXN benchmark, the CRB achieves high performance across splits of increasing difficulty, reaching 99.20% equivalence accuracy on the random split and 91.12% on the extreme out-of-distribution split. SynRBL produces many balanced and chemically plausible completions, but with lower accuracy on the benchmark test splits. Across all methods, performance degrades with increasing incompleteness. We observe a substantial drop when evaluating on reactions outside the benchmark (full uncurated USPTO), highlighting the gap between benchmark performance and practical robustness and motivating future work.

</details>


### [95] [Reinforcement Learning with Markov Risk Measures and Multipattern Risk Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00654)
*Andrzej Ruszczynski,Tiangang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对风险厌恶的有限时域马尔可夫决策问题，提出了一类名为小批量度量的马尔可夫相干风险度量，并定义了多模式风险厌恶问题。基于此，设计了特征驱动的Q学习方法，证明了后悔界为O(H²Nᴴ√K)，并提出了经济版Q学习方法。在随机指派问题和多臂老虎机问题上验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风险厌恶马尔可夫决策问题缺乏高效可处理的风险度量与学习方法，尤其是在有限时域和特征近似场景下，需要既能保证风险相干性又具计算可行性的解决方案。

Method: 提出小批量马尔可夫相干风险度量；定义多模式风险厌恶问题类；设计基于特征的Q学习方法，采用多模式Q因子近似；提出经济版Q学习以简化策略评估（反向）步骤。

Result: 证明了高概率后悔界为O(H²Nᴴ√K)，其中H为时域长度，N为小批量大小，K为回合数。

Conclusion: 提出的方法在理论上具有可证明的后悔界，并在随机指派问题和短时域多臂老虎机问题上验证了有效性。

Abstract: For a risk-averse finite-horizon Markov Decision Problem, we introduce a special class of Markov coherent risk measures, called mini-batch measures. We also define the class of multipattern risk-averse problems that generalizes the class of linear systems. We use both concepts in a feature-based $Q$-learning method with multipattern $Q$-factor approximation and we prove a high-probability regret bound of $\mathcal{O}\big(H^2 N^H \sqrt{ K}\big)$, where $H$ is the horizon, $N$ is the mini-batch size, and $K$ is the number of episodes. We also propose an economical version of the $Q$-learning method that streamlines the policy evaluation (backward) step. The theoretical results are illustrated on a stochastic assignment problem and a short-horizon multi-armed bandit problem.

</details>


### [96] [Bayesian Optimization in Linear Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00237)
*Jesse Schneider,William J. Welch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于递归二元空间划分的自适应贝叶斯优化方法，通过统一建模与采集策略，将计算复杂度从立方级降至线性级，并在6至124维的测试函数上展现出优于标准方法的优化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准贝叶斯优化存在两个主要问题：1) 训练集大小增长时，高斯过程模型训练的计算复杂度为三次方，导致计算不可行；2) 全局建模对于局部优化问题并非最优。本文旨在通过空间划分同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 采用灵活且递归的二元搜索空间划分策略，将标准贝叶斯优化的建模（高斯过程）和采集（获取函数）两个方面与划分方案协调适配，实现局部化建模与采集。

Result: 在7个挑战性测试函数（维度从6到124）上，与常用贝叶斯优化库相比，该方法在所有测试中均取得了更优的优化性能，且计算复杂度为线性。

Conclusion: 通过递归二元空间划分自适应地局部化建模与采集，可同时克服标准贝叶斯优化计算复杂度过高和全局建模不适合局部优化的问题，实现线性复杂度下的更优性能。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization is a sequential method for minimizing objective functions that are expensive to evaluate and about which few assumptions can be made. By using all gathered data to train a Gaussian process model for the function and adaptively employing a mixture of global exploration and local exploitation, this method has been used for optimization in many fields including machine learning, automotive engineering and reinforcement learning. However, the standard method suffers from two problems: 1) with cubic computational complexity in the training-set size it eventually becomes computationally infeasible to train the model, and 2) globally modeling the objective function is not necessarily optimal given the local nature of minimization. Using flexible and recursive binary partitioning of the search space, we adapt both the modeling and acquisitive aspects of standard Bayesian optimization to work harmoniously with the partitioning scheme, thereby ameliorating both standard shortcomings. We compare our method against a commonly used Bayesian optimization library on seven challenging test functions, ranging in dimensionality from $6$ to $124$, and show that our method achieves superior optimization performance in all tests. In addition our method has linear computational complexity.

</details>


### [97] [Pessimism-Free Offline Learning in General-Sum Games via KL Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00264)
*Claire Chen,Yuheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通用和锚定纳什均衡（GANE）与通用和锚定镜像下降（GAMD），证明KL正则化可作为离线多智能体强化学习中独立于悲观惩罚的机制，实现均衡恢复并加速统计收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线多智能体强化学习在一般和设定中面临日志数据集与目标均衡策略之间的分布偏移挑战，现有方法依赖手动悲观惩罚，本文旨在探索KL正则化是否足以稳定学习并恢复均衡。

Method: 提出General-sum Anchored Nash Equilibrium (GANE)恢复正则化纳什均衡，实现$\widetilde{O}(1/n)$加速统计速率；并开发General-sum Anchored Mirror Descent (GAMD)迭代算法，以标准速率$\widetilde{O}(1/\sqrt{n}+1/T)$收敛到粗相关均衡。

Result: GANE实现了$\widetilde{O}(1/n)$的加速统计学习速率；GAMD以$\widetilde{O}(1/\sqrt{n}+1/T)$速率收敛到粗相关均衡。

Conclusion: KL正则化可作为离线多智能体学习中无悲观惩罚的独立机制，在多玩家一般和博弈中达到等效或加速的收敛速率。

Abstract: Offline multi-agent reinforcement learning in general-sum settings is challenged by the distribution shift between logged datasets and target equilibrium policies. While standard methods rely on manual pessimistic penalties, we demonstrate that KL regularization suffices to stabilize learning and achieve equilibrium recovery. We propose General-sum Anchored Nash Equilibrium (GANE), which recovers regularized Nash equilibria at an accelerated statistical rate of $\widetilde{O}(1/n)$. For computational tractability, we develop General-sum Anchored Mirror Descent (GAMD), an iterative algorithm converging to a Coarse Correlated Equilibrium at the standard rate of $\widetilde{O}(1/\sqrt{n}+1/T)$. These results establish KL regularization as a standalone mechanism for pessimism-free offline learning that achieves equivalent or accelerated rates in multi-player general-sum games.

</details>


### [98] [Polaris: Coupled Orbital Polar Embeddings for Hierarchical Concept Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00265)
*Sahil Mishra,Srinitish Srinivasan,Sourish Dasgupta,Tanmoy Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Polaris是一个极坐标超球面嵌入框架，通过角度和半径分离语义与层级结构，在多种分类层次扩展任务（树、多父DAG、多模态层次）上相比14个强基线实现Top-K检索最高提升19点，平均排名降低60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界知识常以层次结构（产品分类、医学本体、标签树）组织，但现有方法因层次结构不对称和语义噪声难以有效学习层次化表示。

Method: 提出Polaris框架：1) 将隐表示投影到北极点切空间，经指数映射映射到球面，使用球面线性层学习单位范数表示；2) 结合鲁棒局部约束、防止几何坍塌的全局正则化、以及不确定性感知的非对称目标函数以鼓励方向包含关系；3) 推理阶段使用结构引导检索缩小候选父节点范围。

Result: 在树、多父DAG、多模态层次等不同分类扩展设置中，相比14个强基线，Top-K检索最高提升约19个点，平均排名降低约60%。

Conclusion: Polaris通过分离语义与层次结构的学习，显著提升了层次化表示的质量，适用于多种复杂层次结构场景。

Abstract: Real-world knowledge is often organized as hierarchies such as product taxonomies, medical ontologies, and label trees, yet learning hierarchical representations is challenging due to asymmetric structure and noisy semantics. We introduce Polaris, a polar hyperspherical embedding framework that separates semanticity from hierarchy using angular geometry and radius, enabling the learning of meaning and structure without interference. To map latent representation onto the sphere, we project it to the tangent space at the north pole, apply the exponential map, and learn unit-norm representations using spherical linear layers. Polaris then combines robust local constraints, global regularization that prevents geometric collapse, and uncertainty-aware asymmetric objectives that encourage directional containment. At inference time, Polaris uses structure-guided retrieval to efficiently narrow down candidate parents before final ranking. We evaluate Polaris on different settings of taxonomy expansion - spanning trees, multi-parent DAGs, and multimodal hierarchies, showing consistent improvements of up to ~19 points in top-K retrieval and up to ~60% reduction in mean rank over fourteen strong baselines.

</details>


### [99] [Jailbroken Frontier Models Retain Their Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00267)
*Daniel Zhu,Zihan Wang,Jenny Bao,Jerry Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明，越先进的模型在遭受越复杂的越狱攻击时，其任务性能下降越少（即"越狱税"与模型能力呈反比）。最强越狱攻击对最先进模型几乎不造成能力损失，因此前沿模型的安全案例不应依赖越狱会显著降低模型能力这一假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究越狱攻击对模型任务性能的"越狱税"是否与模型能力相关，以及评估最先进的越狱攻击是否真的会显著降低模型能力，从而为前沿模型的安全设计提供依据。

Method: 在Claude系列模型（Haiku 4.5到Opus 4.6）上，评估28种越狱攻击在5个基准测试中的表现，分析不同能力层级模型在越狱后的性能下降程度，并对比推理密集型任务与知识回忆型任务的差异。

Result: Haiku 4.5越狱后平均性能下降33.1%，而Opus 4.6在最大思考努力下仅下降7.7%；推理密集型任务比知识回忆型任务退化更严重；最强越狱攻击Boundary Point Jailbreaking实现了近乎完美的分类器规避且性能几乎无下降。

Conclusion: 越狱税与模型能力呈反比，最先进的越狱攻击对最强模型几乎不造成能力损失。前沿模型的安全案例不应依赖越狱会显著降低模型能力这一假设。

Abstract: As language model safeguards become more robust, attackers are pushed toward developing increasingly complex jailbreaks. Prior work has found that this complexity imposes a "jailbreak tax" that degrades the target model's task performance. We show that this tax scales inversely with model capability and that the most advanced jailbreaks effectively yield no reduction in model capabilities. Evaluating 28 jailbreaks on five benchmarks across Claude models ranging in capability from Haiku 4.5 to Opus 4.6, we find Haiku 4.5 loses an average of 33.1% on benchmark performance when jailbroken, while Opus 4.6 at max thinking effort loses only 7.7%. We also observe that across all models, reasoning-heavy tasks display considerably more degradation than knowledge-recall tasks. Finally, Boundary Point Jailbreaking, currently the strongest jailbreak against deployed classifiers, achieves near-perfect classifier evasion with near-zero degradation across safeguarded models. We recommend that safety cases for frontier models should not rely on a meaningful capability degradation from jailbreaks.

</details>


### [100] [A Dirac-Frenkel-Onsager principle: Instantaneous residual minimization with gauge momentum for nonlinear parametrizations of PDE solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00284)
*Matteo Raviola,Benjamin Peherstorfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的正则化方法（Dirac-Frenkel-Onsager动力学），通过引入历史变量（动量）仅沿零空间方向注入，在保持瞬时残差最小化的同时消除参数动力学的病态非唯一性，提高了奇异/近奇异区域的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dirac-Frenkel变分原理在非线性参数化下求解PDE时，参数动力学可能因病态条件而出现非唯一性，传统正则化会引入偏差，因此需要一种既能消除非唯一性又不牺牲瞬时残差最小化精度的方案。

Method: 将参数动力学的非唯一性解释为规范自由度（gauge freedom），基于Onsager最小耗散原理引入历史变量（动量），仅沿零空间方向注入该变量，推导出Dirac-Frenkel-Onsager动力学。

Result: 新方法保留了瞬时残差最小化性质（不同于有偏的标准正则化），同时促进了参数演化的时间平滑性，在奇异和近奇异区域表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过规范自由度注入动量的策略是一种无偏的、有效的病态正则化手段，适用于非线性参数化PDE求解中的奇异性问题。
